# Rockserver Replication Implementation Plan

This plan implements `REPLICATION_ARCHITECTURE.md` with Apache Ratis as the consensus library.

The target is not a Raft demo. The target is a production database stack with predictable correctness, bounded resource use, strong operational recovery, and a clear migration path from embedded Rockserver.

## Guiding Rules

- Use Apache Ratis for consensus, log replication, leader election, membership changes, and snapshots.
- Keep Ratis behind a shaded or classpath-isolated adapter boundary until a JPMS-safe packaging strategy is proven.
- Keep Rockserver responsible for key encoding, partition metadata, placement policy, RocksDB state-machine application, schema, merge semantics, dedupe, CDC, backup, and client behavior.
- Do not maintain a custom production Raft path beside Ratis.
- Preserve embedded and single-server behavior outside replicated mode.
- Do not replicate raw RocksDB WAL as the authoritative history.
- Do not acknowledge replicated writes until the selected Ratis commit/apply durability rule is satisfied.
- Do not allow direct local RocksDB writes in replicated mode.
- Do not let `*_NO_WAL`, direct SST ingestion, or local admin APIs bypass replicated semantics.
- Execute read-modify-write commands inside one partition state machine unless a real transaction protocol exists.
- Commit response dedupe with the affected single-partition mutation.
- Use one dedicated RocksDB database path per partition replica for the first production cut.
- Reject cross-partition multi-key writes in the first production cut.
- Reject local transaction, update, iterator, and embedded CDC handles at the replicated public API boundary.
- Keep deadline, retry-budget, route-cache, and transport metadata out of deterministic state-machine payloads.
- Prefer async, bounded, backpressured APIs on hot paths.
- Define invariants before implementation and enforce them with simulation, crash tests, and black-box tests.

Deliverables:

- [x] Replace the inactive dependency spike with an inactive `ratis-spike` Maven profile.
- [x] Verify the current Apache Ratis release line before pinning.
- [x] Verify Maven dependency tree, JPMS behavior, fatjar behavior, native-image implications, gRPC/Netty conflicts, protobuf conflicts, logging, and metrics dependencies.

- [x] Compile the fatjar profile with `-P fatjar,ratis-spike`.
- [x] Build a minimal classpath or module-path Ratis state-machine probe.
- [x] Define the production Ratis adapter API and classpath/process boundary before any production Ratis state machine is implemented.
- [x] Select the shaded adapter artifact as the production-candidate packaging track while retaining the classpath/process adapter as runtime rehearsal evidence.
- [x] Run a three-peer local Ratis group that commits and queries a toy command.
- [x] Verify the selected Ratis read path for linearizable reads, stale reads, and session/read-your-writes behavior.
- [x] Verify Ratis snapshot save/install hooks for a RocksDB checkpoint-backed state machine.
- [x] Verify Ratis log sync and quorum persistence settings required by `QUORUM_COMMIT`, `QUORUM_COMMIT_APPLY`, and `QUORUM_COMMIT_APPLY_FLUSH`.
- [x] Verify Ratis membership-change APIs for add, remove, leader transfer, and replacement workflows.
- [x] Verify transport choice: `ratis-grpc` first, with `ratis-netty` as a measured alternative only if it is cleaner for Rockserver.
- [x] Document dependency, release, and Java 25/runtime risk before production commitment.

Initial Ratis notes:

- [x] Apache Ratis downloads list 3.2.2 as released on April 3, 2026.
- [x] The Ratis getting-started guide describes Ratis as a Java Raft protocol library, not a standalone database server.
- [x] The getting-started guide shows applications depending on `ratis-server` and one transport such as `ratis-grpc` or `ratis-netty`.
- [x] The inactive Maven profile now pins `org.apache.ratis:ratis-server` and `org.apache.ratis:ratis-grpc` to `${ratis.version}`.
- [x] Dependency note: `ratis-server` 3.2.2 brings `ratis-client`, `ratis-server-api`, `ratis-common`, `ratis-proto`, `ratis-thirdparty-misc`, and `ratis-metrics-api` transitively. The spike should not add `ratis-client` directly unless a later adapter needs an explicit compile boundary.
- [x] Metrics note: `ratis-metrics-default` must be explicit in the classpath spike because Ratis uses `org.apache.ratis.metrics.impl.MetricRegistriesImpl` at runtime and `ratis-server` only declares that artifact for its own tests.
- [x] Compatibility note: Ratis 3.2.2 server artifacts are not JPMS-clean in Rockserver's current Java 25 module build. `ratis-server` and `ratis-server-api` are automatic modules and both contain packages such as `org.apache.ratis.server`, `org.apache.ratis.server.storage`, `org.apache.ratis.server.raftlog`, `org.apache.ratis.server.metrics`, and `org.apache.ratis.server.leader`.
- [x] Read-path note: `RaftServerConfigKeys.Read.Option.LINEARIZABLE` makes ordinary `sendReadOnly` use ReadIndex; `sendReadAfterWrite` waits on the client's write-index cache; `sendStaleRead(message, minIndex, peer)` requires the target server commit index to be at least `minIndex` before invoking `StateMachine.queryStale`.
- [x] Snapshot note: `SnapshotManagementApi.create(...)` triggers the state-machine `takeSnapshot` hook, and a RocksDB checkpoint can be archived as a Ratis `SingleFileSnapshotInfo` snapshot and restored on `StartupOption.RECOVER`.
- [x] Snapshot-transfer note: a three-peer RocksDB snapshot probe can keep one follower offline, create and purge up to a leader snapshot on the two-peer majority, start the lagging follower, and read the checkpointed RocksDB value directly from that follower after Ratis installs the snapshot.
- [x] Durability note: the spike configures the Ratis server to use the segmented disk log, `forceSyncNum=1`, unsafe flush disabled, async flush disabled, log metadata enabled, and state-machine data sync enabled. The default write request uses `ReplicationLevel.MAJORITY`, while the watch API exposes both `MAJORITY` and `MAJORITY_COMMITTED`.
- [x] Membership note: `AdminApi.setConfiguration(...)` with `SetConfigurationRequest.Mode.ADD` adds a new peer, full-list `setConfiguration(...)` removes a peer, `AdminApi.transferLeadership(...)` transfers leadership to an existing voter, and `SetConfigurationRequest.Mode.COMPARE_AND_SET` supports replacement with an expected current configuration.
- [x] Transport note: the spike explicitly selects `SupportedRpcType.GRPC` and all Phase 0 Ratis probes run through `ratis-grpc`. Do not add `ratis-netty` unless a measured packaging or runtime conflict makes gRPC unsuitable for Rockserver.
- [x] Runtime risk note: the forked classpath probe runs on Java 25, but it emits Unsafe warnings from protobuf/gRPC and Ratis third-party Netty plus a native-access warning from Ratis third-party Netty. Ratis 3.2.2 `TimeoutExecutor.getInstance()` uses process-scoped daemon `Timer` threads with no public shutdown hook, so the adapter boundary must own the Ratis JVM lifecycle.
- [x] Native-image note: native-image compatibility is not proven. Do not place Ratis directly into the native image path until a separate adapter process, shaded classpath process, or explicit native-image configuration is tested.
- [x] Dependency-conflict note: the Ratis dependency tree is internally contained through `ratis-thirdparty-misc`, but Rockserver's existing stack still mediates visible gRPC/Netty/protobuf artifacts. The refreshed tree shows `io.grpc:grpc-netty:1.80.0`, `com.google.protobuf:protobuf-java:3.25.8`, and Netty 4.2.12 project dependencies with grpc-netty's older unix-common transitive edge excluded.

- [x] Logging note: the classpath probe now includes `rainbowgum-core`, `rainbowgum-pattern`, and `rainbowgum-slf4j` in the inactive `ratis-spike` profile and sets `logging.level=ERROR` for concise probe output. The production adapter must preserve an explicit SLF4J binding.
- [x] Cleanup note: the probe now runs in a forked classpath JVM through `maven-antrun-plugin` rather than in Maven's process through `exec:java`, so Ratis process-scoped timer threads exit with the probe JVM and Maven no longer reports lingering `timer*` daemon threads. Do not use an in-process `exec:java` run as lifecycle evidence for the adapter.
- [x] Snapshot-transfer note: follower snapshot transfer/install after log purge is covered by the three-peer `snapshot-transfer-node-*` probe.
- [x] Commit-only acknowledgement note: `RaftClient.io().send(...)` returns after leader state-machine apply in Ratis 3.2.2. The first Ratis-backed production cut conservatively maps exact `QUORUM_COMMIT` requests to apply-based acknowledgement through `WriteDurability.applyBasedFallback()` unless a later adapter-level proposal/watch flow proves pre-apply quorum-commit acknowledgement.

Durability matrix:

| Mode | Ratis log and quorum condition | Leader apply | RocksDB local persistence | Response persistence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `QUORUM_COMMIT` | Segmented disk log, `forceSyncNum=1`, unsafe and async flush disabled, log metadata enabled, and `MAJORITY_COMMITTED` reached for the log index. | Not required by the mode, but ordinary Ratis `send` waits for apply; use a stricter apply path until a pre-apply adapter flow exists. | No RocksDB write is required before acknowledgement. | Not allowed for response-producing writes. |
| `QUORUM_COMMIT_APPLY` | Same Ratis log/quorum settings as `QUORUM_COMMIT`. | Required before acknowledgement. | User data, `lastApplied`, and dedupe metadata must be persisted in one RocksDB write batch with WAL enabled. | Required for response-producing writes before acknowledgement. |
| `QUORUM_COMMIT_APPLY_FLUSH` | Same Ratis log/quorum settings as `QUORUM_COMMIT_APPLY`. | Required before acknowledgement. | Same write batch as `QUORUM_COMMIT_APPLY`, plus explicit RocksDB flush/checkpoint action before acknowledgement. | Required for response-producing writes before acknowledgement. |

Apache Ratis production decision:

- [x] Conditional go: use Apache Ratis as the replicated substrate for later phases only behind the classpath-isolated adapter boundary.
- [x] No-go for direct in-module JPMS integration in the current build: Ratis 3.2.2 split packages break the Java 25 module/fatjar path.
- [x] No-go for native-image integration until the adapter boundary is a separate classpath process or a dedicated native-image spike proves the required reflection, initialization, threading, gRPC, and RocksDB interactions.
- [x] Production adapter prerequisites: preserve the explicit SLF4J binding, keep Ratis timer/executor threads owned by a process-scoped adapter lifecycle, complete dependency mediation review, and implement a custom proposal/watch path before exposing exact pre-apply `QUORUM_COMMIT` acknowledgement.

Exit criteria:

- [x] A minimal Ratis state machine starts in-process.
- [x] A three-peer local group commits and reads a command.
- [x] The dependency tree is compatible with the current build or the required adapter boundary is defined and used by all later phases.
- [x] The durability matrix documents Ratis log sync, quorum commit, leader apply, RocksDB WAL, explicit flush, and response persistence.
- [x] Snapshot and restore hooks are proven with a RocksDB-backed toy state machine.
- [x] A written go/no-go decision exists for Apache Ratis as the replicated substrate.

## Phase 1: Command And API Audit

Classify every `RocksDBAPICommand` against Ratis state-machine semantics.

Deliverables:

- [x] A command classification table:
  - [x] replicated single-key write;
  - [x] replicated multi-key write;
  - [x] replicated metadata write;
  - [x] linearizable read;
  - [x] stale/local read;
  - [x] stream/range read;
  - [x] local-only;
  - [x] unsupported in replicated mode.
- [x] A list of commands containing local handles: transaction IDs, iterator IDs, update IDs, and stream cursors.
- [x] A public-boundary rejection table for local handles in replicated mode.
- [x] A list of response-producing writes requiring dedupe.
- [x] A list of read-modify-write commands that must execute inside one partition state machine.
- [x] A list of WAL/SST/unsafe modes that must be rejected or converted.
- [x] Merge operator determinism requirements.
- [x] A deterministic command-envelope spec that excludes deadline, retry-budget, route-cache, and transport metadata from state-machine payloads.
- [x] A `RequestType` compatibility table covering `NOTHING`, `PREVIOUS`, `CURRENT`, `FOR_UPDATE`, `EXISTS`, `DELTA`, `MULTI`, `CHANGED`, `PREVIOUS_PRESENCE`, `MERGED`, `FIRST_AND_LAST`, `ALL_IN_RANGE`, and `ENTRIES_COUNT`.
- [x] A command safety table that does not rely only on `RocksDBAPICommand.isReadOnly()`.
- [x] A first-cut rule that `putMulti`, `deleteMulti`, `mergeMulti`, `putBatch`, and `mergeBatch` are accepted only when prevalidated as single-partition commands.
- [x] A transport parity table for Java API, gRPC/protobuf, Thrift if retained, and the Rust client.
- [x] An inventory of existing custom Raft/MicroRaft tests and codecs; the custom Raft/MicroRaft implementations and RPCs have since been deleted, while command codec/API coverage remains.

Evidence: `REPLICATION_COMMAND_AUDIT.md`.

- [x] `mvn -Dtest=ReplicatedGrpcBoundaryTest,ReplicatedWriteSyncAPITest,ReplicatedCommandClassifierTest,ClusterRequestRouterTest test` verifies Java API, route classification, and gRPC/protobuf local-handle rejection before Ratis proposal.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=ReplicatedGrpcBoundaryTest,ReplicatedThriftBoundaryTest,ReplicatedWriteSyncAPITest,ReplicatedCommandClassifierTest,ClusterRequestRouterTest test` verifies Java API, route classification, gRPC/protobuf, and Thrift local-handle rejection before Ratis proposal.
- [x] `cargo test -p rockserver-client` verifies the Rust client exposes public parsing for Rockserver gRPC `RocksDBError: [uid:...]` statuses, including `NOT_IMPLEMENTED`.

- [x] `mvn -Dtest=ReplicatedCommandClassifierTest,ReplicatedWriteSyncAPITest,ClusterRequestRouterTest test` verifies every first-cut replicated write and metadata write has an explicit retry/dedupe policy.

Exit criteria:

- [x] Replicated mode can reject unsupported commands before they reach Ratis.
- [x] Java replicated write API and route classification reject local handles at the public API/routing layer.
- [x] gRPC/protobuf paths reject local handles with equivalent public errors.
- [x] Thrift paths reject local handles with equivalent public errors.
- [x] Rust client paths receive and decode equivalent public Rockserver errors over gRPC/protobuf.
- [x] Every retryable write command has a response and dedupe policy.

Build Rockserver's distributed ownership layer.

- [x] Preserve `ColumnInstance.calculateKey` behavior exactly, including bucketed-column semantics.
- [x] Define partition-boundary rules for bucketed columns: route by calculated key and never split collision buckets.
- [x] Define schema prepare/activate/fence metadata commands for create/delete column and merge-operator activation.

## Phase 3: Ratis State Machine

Implement the data-partition state machine.

Deliverables:

- [x] Add `RockserverRatisStateMachine`.
- [x] Define protobuf or binary command payloads for partition writes.
- [x] Ensure command payloads contain only deterministic replay inputs and never deadline, retry-budget, routing, or transport metadata.
- [x] Implement deterministic command validation and schema epoch checks.
- [x] Implement apply for blind put/delete.
- [x] Implement read-modify-write apply for merge and response-producing writes.
- [x] Implement single-partition `putMulti`, `deleteMulti`, and `mergeMulti`; reject cross-partition variants before Ratis.
- [x] Persist user mutation, dedupe metadata, and `lastApplied` metadata atomically enough for replay and snapshot correctness.
- [x] Implement client lease and acknowledged-watermark storage for dedupe expiry.
- [x] Return explicit unknown-outcome/dedupe-expired errors when a retry arrives after its dedupe record was legitimately expired.
- [x] Map replicated `WriteDurability` to the verified Ratis/RocksDB durability matrix.
- [x] Reject embedded-only local durability shortcuts in replicated mode.
- [x] Reject local `mergeOperatorClass` shortcuts in replicated mode; use only metadata-activated merge artifacts or built-in deterministic operators.
- [x] Add failure injection around append, commit, apply, RocksDB write, response persistence, and response send.

Exit criteria:

- [x] Duplicate `(clientId, requestId)` never applies twice.
- [x] Replayed logs produce identical RocksDB state.
- [x] Crash after commit before response returns the original response on retry.
- [x] Response-producing writes always wait for leader apply.
- [x] Per-partition RocksDB checkpoint/reopen preserves `lastApplied`, data, and dedupe state.

## Phase 4: Server Lifecycle, Routing, And Client API

Wire Ratis into Rockserver runtime.

Deliverables:

- [x] Add replicated-mode configuration for node ID, advertised Ratis endpoint, public API endpoint, data paths, metadata peers, and failure domain.
- [x] Start and stop Ratis servers with Rockserver lifecycle.
- [x] Add partition route cache with metadata epoch validation.
- [x] Route writes to the owning Ratis group leader.
- [x] Handle `NotLeader`, stale route, timeout, duplicate retry, and deadline expiry.
- [x] Add per-partition admission queues, batching, and backpressure.
- [x] Keep deadlines and retry budgets in gateway admission logic, not in replicated payload semantics.
- [x] Reject transaction IDs, update IDs, iterator IDs, embedded CDC handles, `*_NO_WAL`, and direct SST ingestion before command encoding in replicated mode.
- [x] Separate public API endpoints from internal Ratis endpoints.
- [x] Remove or isolate existing custom Raft RPCs from the production data API.
- [x] Bridge external-process adapter RPC operations to and from `RatisPartitionClient` without placing Ratis on the production module path, including a reusable adapter-side socket RPC server.
- [x] Materialize process adapter partition config from `ClusterMetadata` for the local voting partitions without importing Apache Ratis into the production module.
- [x] Build a process adapter launch plan from metadata that writes the generated config, creates the classpath-isolated adapter command, and exposes config-writing lifecycle/client-factory helpers over fixed health/RPC endpoints.
- [x] Add a process adapter metadata file codec for the cluster metadata subset needed by adapter-side Rockserver/RocksDB state-machine construction.
- [x] Wire the classpath adapter entrypoint to consume generated config plus metadata, start adapter runtimes with real Rockserver/RocksDB state machines when metadata is provided, and expose those partition clients through the socket RPC handler.
- [x] Add a shared server-side Ratis lifecycle wrapper so one adapter process can be attached to multiple public endpoints without duplicate starts and closes only after the last endpoint stops.
- [x] Add replicated-mode adapter process settings for the adapter home, manifest, Java executable, health endpoint, and RPC endpoint, then derive the process launch plan/shared lifecycle/adapter factory from `ReplicatedModeConfiguration` plus `ClusterMetadata`.
- [x] Project `BootstrapPlan` into initial `ClusterMetadata` so first-start adapter launch can be built from bootstrap peers before any data partitions exist.
- [x] Add a strict file codec for `BootstrapManifest`, expose `bootstrap-manifest-path` in replicated-mode config, and let the server adapter factory build first-start launch plans from the configured bootstrap manifest.
- [x] Wire `Main` to attach the configured shared bootstrap Ratis lifecycle to both public server builders when replicated mode is enabled.
- [x] Add an adapter-backed client resolver that exposes one RPC-backed `RatisPartitionClient` map through the write, point-read, and range-read route submitter resolver interfaces.
- [x] Wire the adapter-level `RELOAD_METADATA` RPC to safe classpath adapter runtime reload of the generated config and metadata files, including in-place metadata refresh for unchanged Rockserver state machines, added-partition startup before client exposure, removed-partition shutdown, and explicit rejection of running descriptor changes until partition membership workflows own them.

- [x] `mvn -Dtest=RatisProcessAdapterConfigTest,RatisProcessAdapterDeploymentPreflightTest,RatisProcessAdapterServerLifecycleFactoryTest test` verifies an explicit deployment preflight for real adapter executable/classpath files, writable generated config/metadata and partition data paths, non-overlapping storage directories, listener separation including adapter health/RPC collisions with local Ratis peer listeners and loopback aliases, environment/config path consistency, duplicate peer endpoint rejection, descriptor-level `grpc` endpoint enforcement, structured `ratis-adapter-preflight.properties` report publication, deterministic `ratis-adapter-evidence.properties` launch-evidence generation with launch-file SHA-256/size fingerprints plus canonical generated config/metadata hashes for supervisor archival, preflight-gated evidence publication, and the configured lifecycle-factory entry point for running the preflight from replicated-mode settings.

- [x] `mvn -Dtest=RatisProcessAdapterDeploymentPreflightTest test` verifies preflight report and launch-evidence artifact writes enforce byte caps and leave existing archival files untouched when an oversized artifact is rejected.

Exit criteria:

- [ ] A production Ratis-backed multi-node Rockserver cluster can create metadata, create a partition, put, get, and survive leader restart. The generated-distribution local external-process adapter rehearsal now covers put/get plus leader restart using pre-published metadata; public `createColumn`/`deleteColumn` can drive a configured adapter-boundary Ratis metadata submitter; modeled metadata commands have a checksummed and size-bounded log payload codec; a reusable metadata-log submitter can propose encoded commands to a log client and read back committed metadata; the classpath probe proves a real Ratis metadata-group state machine/client can drive `ReplicatedConnection` create-column, register-node, and create-partition workflows; configured launch plans now include an adapter-internal metadata group descriptor; classpath metadata-group snapshot recovery is proven for a restarted snapshot peer; generated live metadata is rejected as an unsafe no-snapshot replay seed by an explicit startup policy; initial node registration plus partition creation now has a `ReplicatedConnection` workflow that submits metadata commands, publishes both adapter-consumed partition descriptors and route groups, and refreshes routing before a subsequent write; and configured launch plans have a deployment preflight plus deterministic launch-evidence artifact for filesystem, endpoint, listener-collision, supervisor-command, launch-file fingerprint, generated config/metadata identity, and adapter-home isolation invariants. This remains blocked on a deployed-environment supervisor/network/storage rehearsal.
- [x] Client retries are idempotent and budgeted.
- [x] Unsupported local-handle APIs fail explicitly through Java API, gRPC/protobuf, Thrift if retained, and Rust client paths.

## Phase 5: Reads, Scans, And Iterators

Define replicated read semantics.

Deliverables:

- [x] Implement default linearizable point reads through the verified Ratis read path.
- [x] Implement explicit stale/local reads with clear API flags and safety checks.
- [x] Implement linearizable single-partition range scans through the verified per-partition read path.
- [x] Implement multi-partition range scans as metadata-epoch-consistent, partition-consistent, non-atomic scans.
- [x] Implement resumable distributed cursors.
- [x] Define behavior when partitions split during scans.
- [x] Bound memory, RPC fanout, and server-side iterator lifetime.
- [x] Keep replicated cursors stateless/resumable; do not expose local server-side iterator handles.

Phase 5 verification log:

- [x] `mvn -Dtest=PartitionPointReadSubmitterTest,ClusterRequestRouterTest,PartitionRouteCacheTest,ReplicatedCommandClassifierTest test` verifies deterministic point-read query payload encoding/decoding, metadata-epoch and schema-epoch validation, calculated-key partition ownership, leader routing for default linearizable reads only when the leader is serving reads, deadline/stale-route rejection before query dispatch, and use of `RatisPartitionClient.queryLinearizable(...)` without proposing to the replicated write log.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=PartitionPointReadSubmitterTest,ClusterRequestRouterTest,PartitionRouteCacheTest,ReplicatedCommandClassifierTest test` verifies explicit point-read `RoutingPolicy` support for follower/stale and local reads, including minimum-applied-index replica selection, rejection when no replica is fresh enough, dispatch to `RatisPartitionClient.queryStale(...)` or `queryLocal(...)`, and no fallback to the write proposal path.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=PartitionReadCommandCodecTest,PartitionPointReadSubmitterTest,PartitionRangeReadSubmitterTest,ReplicatedConnectionTest test` verifies point-read and range-read command codecs now bound query bodies and key-part byte fields on encode and decode, rejecting malformed inbound key lengths before large allocation while preserving point/range routing and public replicated point-read coverage.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=PartitionRangeReadSubmitterTest,PartitionBoundaryRulesTest,PartitionPointReadSubmitterTest,ClusterRequestRouterTest,PartitionRouteCacheTest,ReplicatedCommandClassifierTest test` verifies deterministic single-partition range-read query payload encoding/decoding, half-open partition-boundary validation, rejection of cross-partition and open-ended multi-partition scans before client resolution, metadata/schema/deadline rejection, leader routing for linearizable scans, and use of `RatisPartitionClient.queryLinearizable(...)` without proposing to the replicated write log.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=PartitionRangeReadSubmitterTest,PartitionBoundaryRulesTest,PartitionPointReadSubmitterTest,ClusterRequestRouterTest,PartitionRouteCacheTest,ReplicatedCommandClassifierTest test` verifies metadata-epoch-consistent multi-partition range scan planning, contiguous partition coverage validation, calculated-key segment ordering, reverse segment ordering, open interior segment bounds, stale metadata rejection, gap rejection before client resolution, ordered per-partition payload collection, and no fallback to the write proposal path.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=PartitionRangeReadSubmitterTest,PartitionBoundaryRulesTest,PartitionPointReadSubmitterTest,ClusterRequestRouterTest,PartitionRouteCacheTest,ReplicatedCommandClassifierTest test` verifies stateless distributed scan cursor token encoding/decoding, checksum rejection, cursor opening without Ratis client resolution, page-by-page resume at partition segment boundaries, reverse cursor resume order, stale cursor epoch rejection, invalid segment index rejection, metadata gap rejection before client resolution, and no server-side iterator/local-handle dependency.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=PartitionRangeScanCursorCodecTest,PartitionRangeReadSubmitterTest,PartitionReadCommandCodecTest test` verifies stateless scan cursor tokens now bound cursor bodies and embedded range-command fields on encode and decode, rejecting malformed inbound embedded-command lengths before large allocation while preserving cursor round-trip and page-resume coverage.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=PartitionRangeReadSubmitterTest,PartitionBoundaryRulesTest,PartitionPointReadSubmitterTest,ClusterRequestRouterTest,PartitionRouteCacheTest,ReplicatedCommandClassifierTest test` verifies single-partition scans, multi-partition scans, and cursor resumes reject `PartitionSplitState` values other than `ACTIVE` before Ratis client resolution, so clients get a clean metadata refresh/reopen boundary during partition movement.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=PartitionRangeReadSubmitterTest,PartitionBoundaryRulesTest,PartitionPointReadSubmitterTest,ClusterRequestRouterTest,PartitionRouteCacheTest,ReplicatedCommandClassifierTest test` verifies configured scan resource limits: cursor pages clamp requested segment count to the configured fanout, non-cursor wide scans reject fanout above the configured page limit, oversized segment responses fail with `RESOURCE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`, and stateless cursors keep server-side iterator lifetime at zero.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=PartitionPointReadSubmitterTest,PartitionRangeReadSubmitterTest,PartitionBoundaryRulesTest,ClusterRequestRouterTest,PartitionRouteCacheTest,ReplicatedCommandClassifierTest test` verifies linearizable point reads re-resolve after a leader metadata-epoch change, read the pre-change committed value from the old leader, read the post-change committed value from the new leader, and never fall back to the write proposal path.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=ReplicatedConnectionTest test` verifies public replicated point reads decode checksummed single-value adapter responses, preserve empty `Buf` values separately from missing `null` values, expose `RequestType.exists()` through the Ratis query path, and still reject unsupported public operations before adapter dispatch.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=PartitionPointReadCommandValidatorTest,PartitionPointReadSubmitterTest,PartitionReadCommandCodecTest,PartitionBoundaryRulesTest,ReplicatedConnectionTest test` and `mvn -P library,ratis-spike -DskipTests compile antrun:run@run-ratis-classpath-probe` verify gateway routing and real classpath data-partition state machines share point-read schema/key validation, replica-local reads reject calculated keys outside the local half-open partition range before RocksDB access, and an opaque adapter query cannot bypass gateway ownership routing.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=PartitionPointReadSubmitterTest test` verifies unsupported adapter stale/local point-read modes return typed `QUERY_FAILED` route failures with policy-specific diagnostics instead of leaking raw unsupported-operation exceptions.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=PartitionRangeReadSubmitterTest test` verifies unsupported linearizable range-query adapter modes return typed `QUERY_FAILED` route failures for both synchronous and future-completed unsupported operations instead of leaking raw unsupported-operation exceptions.

Exit criteria:

- [x] Linearizable reads observe committed writes across leader changes.
- [x] Single-partition scans can be linearizable.
- [x] Wide scans document and test their non-atomic consistency contract, resume after client disconnect, and restart or fail cleanly on incompatible partition movement.

## Phase 6: Snapshots, Backup, Restore, And CDC

Make recovery and observability production-grade.

Deliverables:

- [x] Implement Ratis snapshot save/load using RocksDB checkpoints plus metadata manifests.
- [x] Include partition ID, term/index, schema epoch, dedupe watermark, CDC watermark, and checksums in snapshots.
- [x] Implement snapshot install tests for lagging peers and replacement peers.
- [x] Define backup manifests as replicated metadata with metadata backup epoch and per-partition term/index cuts.
- [x] Implement partition-parallel backup with rate limits and checksums.
- [x] Implement restore into a new cluster or new partition epoch, never silent local overwrite.
- [x] Emit CDC from the replicated apply path.
- [x] Add CDC resume tokens shaped as `(metadataEpoch, partitionId, partitionEpoch, appliedIndex, eventOrdinal)`.
- [x] Define cluster CDC as a merge of partition-local streams with no total cross-partition order in the first production cut.
- [x] Add split/merge token translation records to metadata.

Phase 6 verification log:

- [x] `mvn -Dtest=RockserverRatisSnapshotSupportTest,RocksDBCheckpointSnapshotSupportTest test` verifies Ratis partition snapshots prepend a checksummed metadata manifest to RocksDB checkpoint chunks, carry partition ID, partition epoch, Ratis term/index, metadata/schema epochs, dedupe and CDC watermarks, validate checkpoint manifest checksums before install, reject wrong partition/index installs, and restore a real RocksDB checkpoint into a replacement directory.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=RockserverRatisSnapshotSupportTest,RockserverRatisStateMachineTest,RockserverRatisStateMachinePersistentBatchTest,RocksDBCheckpointSnapshotSupportTest test` verifies replacement-peer snapshot install followed by lagging log replay: a restored state machine starts from persisted `lastApplied`, rejects duplicate snapshot-index entries, applies later committed entries, and advances both in-memory and persisted `lastApplied`.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=MetadataCommandTest test` verifies backup manifests as typed replicated metadata commands, including metadata backup epoch fences, canonical per-partition term/index cuts, duplicate partition-cut rejection, and proof that each partition cut observed the manifest backup epoch.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=MetadataCommandTest test` passes after making backup manifest IDs reject line-broken values before replicated metadata command construction.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=PartitionBackupExecutorTest,MetadataCommandTest test` verifies partition-parallel backup execution with a configured concurrency limit, per-partition byte limit, Ratis snapshot/cut validation, deterministic artifact ordering, and aggregate snapshot checksums derived from the Ratis manifest, RocksDB checkpoint manifest, and file chunk checksums.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=PartitionBackupExecutorTest,BackupRestoreExecutorTest,RockserverRatisSnapshotSupportTest test` verifies partition backup checksum aggregation now streams snapshot descriptor bytes into CRC32C without materializing an intermediate aggregate byte array while preserving backup/restore planning and snapshot manifest validation coverage.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=PartitionBackupExecutorTest,BackupRestoreExecutorTest test` verifies partition backup export rejects oversized manifest partition-cut counts before exporter invocation and submits snapshot work through a rolling window capped by the configured concurrency limit.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=BackupRestoreExecutorTest,PartitionBackupExecutorTest test` verifies restore planning recomputes artifact byte totals and aggregate snapshot CRC32C from supplied chunks, rejecting internally inconsistent artifacts before any installer side effects.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=PartitionBackupExecutorTest,BackupRestoreExecutorTest test` verifies partition backup checksum traversal rejects file chunks whose CRC32C does not match their bytes before export checksum publication or restore installer side effects.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=BackupRestoreExecutorTest test` verifies restore targets reject null partition IDs, null epoch values, and negative partition epochs before restore planning or map copying.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=PartitionBackupExecutorTest,BackupRestoreExecutorTest test` verifies backup artifact and restore-plan checksum fields reject out-of-range CRC32C values before backup/restore planning.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=BackupRestoreExecutorTest,PartitionBackupExecutorTest,RockserverRatisSnapshotSupportTest test` verifies restore planning and execution into a new cluster target, real RocksDB snapshot install from a backup artifact, schema key preservation, user data preservation, dedupe watermark preservation, CDC position preservation, same-cluster restore rejection unless metadata and partition epochs advance, missing/mismatched artifact rejection before installer execution, and source manifest cut exposure for audit.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=RockserverRatisStateMachinePersistentBatchTest,PartitionStateWriteBatchTest,RocksDBPartitionStateWriteBatchWriterTest,RockserverStorageKeyspaceTest test` verifies replicated CDC events are persisted from the committed apply path in the same RocksDB batch as user data, dedupe metadata, and `lastApplied`; resume tokens contain metadata epoch, partition ID, partition epoch, applied index, and event ordinal; duplicate-request replay does not emit a second CDC event.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=ReplicatedCdcEventCodecTest,RockserverRatisStateMachinePersistentBatchTest,ClusterCdcStreamMergerTest,BackupRestoreExecutorTest test` verifies replicated CDC event payloads now bound event bodies and key/value byte fields on encode and decode, rejecting malformed inbound key lengths before large allocation while preserving persistent CDC, stream merge, and backup/restore coverage.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=ClusterCdcStreamMergerTest,RockserverRatisStateMachinePersistentBatchTest test` verifies cluster CDC page construction as a merge of strictly ordered partition-local streams, preserves per-partition ordering, intentionally does not impose applied-index ordering across partitions, and returns per-partition resume tokens only for partitions that advanced in the page.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=ClusterCdcStreamMergerTest,RockserverRatisStateMachinePersistentBatchTest,ReplicatedCdcEventCodecTest test` verifies cluster CDC page merging rejects non-positive and oversized `maxEvents` values before result-list allocation while preserving committed CDC persistence and event codec coverage.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=MetadataCommandTest,ClusterCdcStreamMergerTest test` verifies split and merge CDC token translation records as replicated metadata commands, including child/parent partition validation, metadata-epoch fencing, child partition-epoch advancement, and command-kind coverage.

Exit criteria:

- [x] A new peer can catch up from snapshot and log replay.
- [x] Backup and restore preserve schema, data, dedupe watermarks, and CDC position.
- [x] Backup restore exposes the source manifest's per-partition cuts for audit.

## Phase 7: Membership, Placement, And Rebalancing

Build the placement layer Ratis does not provide as a database service.

Deliverables:

- [x] Add node health and capacity reporting.
- [x] Add placement policies for voters across failure domains.
- [x] Add leader balancing.
- [x] Add partition split and merge workflows.
- [x] Add add-replica, catch-up, promote, remove-replica, and leader-transfer workflows.
- [x] Verify whether Ratis listener/observer roles can be used for non-voting catch-up in the selected release.
- [x] If listener/observer behavior is not proven, block unsafe online replacement paths and allow only conservative voter changes while an existing quorum is healthy.
- [x] Add hot-partition detection and manual/admin rebalancing controls.

Phase 7 verification log:

- [x] `mvn -Dtest=NodeHealthReportTest test` verifies node health reports validate resource metrics, freshness, disk and memory utilization, and replica-eligibility decisions for healthy, stale, and unavailable nodes.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=VoterPlacementPolicyTest,ClusterMetadataTest test` verifies stored cluster node metadata, deterministic voter selection from fresh healthy capacity reports, spreading voters across required failure domains, balanced fill for extra voters, and validation of existing voter placements.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=LeaderBalancePolicyTest test` verifies advisory leader-transfer planning from overloaded leaders to fresh healthy caught-up voters, transfer caps with projected leader counts, rejection of lagging, learner, and stale targets, and skipping partitions in split/merge states.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=PartitionTopologyWorkflowPlannerTest test` verifies split and merge workflow planning with metadata-epoch fences, partition-epoch fences, strict split boundaries, adjacent merge parents, fresh child/merged partition IDs, conservative merge membership checks, and CDC token translation records for the resulting metadata epoch.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=PartitionMembershipWorkflowPlannerTest test` verifies add-replica as learner, registered-node admission when node metadata is available, catch-up targets from the current leader, promotion only after catch-up, removal with old-quorum and serving replacement-leader constraints, and leader transfer only to caught-up serving voters.
- [x] Local Ratis 3.2.2 artifact inspection verifies `RaftPeerRole` exposes `LISTENER` but not `OBSERVER`, `RaftPeer.Builder` supports listener startup role, and `SetConfigurationRequest`/`RaftConfiguration` model listeners as non-voting configuration members; no local proof currently demonstrates listener catch-up plus safe promotion.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=RatisNonVotingCatchUpAssessmentTest test` verifies the classpath assessment remains conservative without a catch-up/promotion proof and `OnlineReplacementSafetyPolicy` blocks non-voting replacement while allowing conservative voter changes only when an existing quorum is healthy.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=HotPartitionRebalanceControlsTest test` verifies fresh hot-partition detection across operation rate, write rate, byte rate, latency, and queue-depth signals; stale reports are ignored; explicit admin rebalance requests require metadata epoch, partition epoch, active topology state, reason text, and concurrency admission.
- [x] `mvn -Dtest=NodeHealthReportTest,VoterPlacementPolicyTest,LeaderBalancePolicyTest,PartitionTopologyWorkflowPlannerTest,PartitionMembershipWorkflowPlannerTest,RatisNonVotingCatchUpAssessmentTest,HotPartitionRebalanceControlsTest,ClusterMetadataTest test` verifies the complete Phase 7 metadata/planner policy set together.

Exit criteria:

- [x] Node replacement does not lose acknowledged writes at the metadata workflow layer: replacement plans require caught-up peers before promotion/removal, preserve old quorum size for voter removal, require caught-up serving replacement leaders, and block unproven non-voting Ratis catch-up paths.
- [x] Rebalancing preserves quorum and placement constraints at the planner layer: voter placement enforces failure-domain spread, leader transfer targets caught-up voters only, split/merge plans are epoch fenced, and admin rebalancing is explicit, concurrency-limited, and topology-state fenced.

Phase 7 exit criteria are satisfied for metadata and planner behavior. Phase 8 remains responsible for deterministic simulation, crash testing, and black-box proof across live execution.

## Phase 8: Testing And Release Qualification

Prove correctness and operational behavior before enabling production.

Deliverables:

- [x] Deterministic simulation for partitions, network partitions, disk failures, restarts, duplicate retries, and delayed snapshots.

- [x] Upgrade and downgrade tests for metadata and command versions.
- [x] Dependency and license scan for Ratis profile.
- [x] Java 25 runtime and native-image compatibility report.
- [x] Performance tests for:
  - [x] write throughput per Store;
  - [x] read throughput per Store;
  - [x] partition count;
  - [x] snapshot transfer;
  - [x] restore;
  - [x] large range scans;
  - [x] compaction debt;
  - [x] network backpressure.

- [x] `mvn -Dtest=JepsenStyleBlackBoxReadConsistencyTest test` verifies a black-box operation history for put, get, delete, and merge with read-consistency checking against all writes completed before each read begins, and rejects a stale read after a completed write.

- [x] Baseline dependency-tree checks, the isolated adapter artifact copy list, and local POM/JAR metadata inspection are recorded in `REPLICATION_RATIS_DEPENDENCY_LICENSE_SCAN.md`; the scan finds no new Ratis-profile license blocker, keeps notice preservation mandatory, and identifies existing JPMS/module-name risks that keep the adapter boundary required.
